On Friday evening, after "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart", there will be no Lenten troparia with prostrations, but the Troparion of the Saint, Glory, Both now: Theotokion from the Sunday ones, in the tone of the Troparion of the Saint[^651] and, after the litany "Have mercy on us, O God", - 3 great prostrations[^535] and the Great Dismissal, as usual
##### 4.1.8.8 If on this day falls a saint with Polyeleos, namely the Finding of the Precious Head of the Forerunner and the 40 Martyrs, and also on Monday, Tuesday and Wednesday of Passion Week[^536], then immediately after "Let my prayer be set forth" there will be censing and on the two previously named feasts their Epistle and Gospel are read, the Gospel - beginning from "Wisdom, Aright". On the three days of Passion Week only the Gospel is read, beginning from "And that we may be accounted worthy", since there is no Epistle
##### 4.1.8.9 By the decision of the Synod of Lviv, the pastor must celebrate the Liturgy of the Presanctified on every Wednesday and every Friday of Great Lent and on Monday, Tuesday and Wednesday of Passion Week
Note on the number of prostrations per day. The number of prostrations made in church reached once 300 per day, excluding the Midnight Office: "Three hundred during the day and night, without the Midnight Office". (Greek Triodion, our Manuscript Typikon and Tserkovne Oko on the 1st Monday of Lent).

--- Match 21 [Terms: monasteries, in monasteries] ---
Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday and Friday
of the fourth week of Great Lent
Everything - according to the general Lenten rubric of the previous weeks, except the veneration of the Precious Cross, according to the previously stated. Only Wednesday and Friday differ from the other three days in that at Matins, instead of the Canon of the Menaion, they have the Cross Canon of the Triodion. Also at Vespers on Tuesday for Wednesday all 6 stichera on "Lord, I have cried" will be of the Cross; and the Canon of the Menaion on these two days we take at Compline.
### 4.1.15 Fourth Saturday of Great Lent
Everything - according to the rubric of the second Saturday of Great Lent, here on page 357.
### 4.1.16 Fourth Sunday of Great Lent
Everything - according to the rubric of the second Sunday of Great Lent, here on page 360.
Note
The service of St. John of the Ladder, which some later [typika] prescribed to sing on this Sunday, is usually not taken among us. And if someone, namely in monasteries, wishes to sing it, let him sing it instead of the service of the regular saint of the Menaion, according to the rubric for a saint without Polyeleos on 4, and let him take the service of the regular saint at Compline. According to this Typikon, the service of St. Mary of Egypt is sung on the fifth[^550] Sunday of Lent, but if someone wishes, he may sing it instead of the service of the regular saint.

--- Match 22 [Terms: monasteries, According to our custom] ---
### 4.2.1 Lazarus Saturday
Note
The service of this feast is of the small type and only at Matins, from the Canon onwards, - great. However, also before the Canon it has some parts of the Sunday service, namely after the 2nd Kathisma, that is "The Blameless", it has "The Choir of angels", after the 2nd Sessional Hymn it has "The Resurrection of Christ" and before the Exaposteilarion - "Holy is the Lord our God" three times.
RUBRIC
On Friday evening
AT VESPERS WITH PRESANCTIFIED
##### 4.2.1.1 Kathisma proper
##### 4.2.1.2 On "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: Idiomelon of the day twice, Martyricon once, then 7 of the feast, Glory, Both now: of the feast
##### 4.2.1.3 At "O Gladsome Light" - Prokimenon of the Triodion and one reading of the Triodion, then 2nd Prokimenon, 2nd reading of the Triodion and everything else of the Presanctified
AT Small Compline
We sing the Canon of Lazarus in the Triodion, after the Canon, instead of "It is truly meet", - Heirmos of the 9th Ode of the Canon. After the Trisagion - Kontakion of the feast only.
AT THE Midnight Office
After the 1st Trisagion, instead of the troparia "Uncreated Nature", we read the Troparion of Lazarus; after the 2nd Trisagion, instead of the troparia "Remember, O Lord", - Kontakion of Lazarus, and the prayer "Remember" we do not say.
AT MATINS
##### 4.2.1.4 On "God is the Lord": Troparion of the feast twice, Glory, Both now: the same
##### 4.2.1.5 After the 1st and 2nd Kathismata - Sessional Hymn of the feast, however, after the 2nd Kathisma, before the Sessional Hymn, we sing "The Choir of angels" exactly as on Sunday; and after the Sessional Hymn - "The Resurrection of Christ", Psalm 50 and immediately Canon
##### 4.2.1.6 Canons of the feast - two: 1st - with heirmos on 8 and 2nd - on 6. From the 6th Ode - tetraodion, heirmos - twice, troparia - on 6, then the other canon on 6. Katavasia after each ode - Heirmos of the 2nd Canon. Refrain to the troparia - "Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee". After the 3rd Ode - Sessional Hymn, after the 6th - Kontakion and Ikos of the feast; at the 9th we do not sing "More honorable"; after the 9th we do not say "It is truly meet" until the Sunday of Ap. Thomas; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the feast, however, before the Exaposteilarion we sing "Holy is the Lord our God" three times
##### 4.2.1.7 Stichera of the Praises - 8: all - of the feast, with two Sunday verses, as every Sunday; Glory: of the feast, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou"
##### 4.2.1.8 After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the feast only
##### 4.2.1.9 Dismissal specific
AT THE HOURS AND AT THE LITURGY: Everything - only of the feast, and at the Liturgy, instead of the Trisagion, we sing "All Who have been baptized into Christ".
### 4.2.2 Flower Sunday (Palm Sunday)
[^562]Everything - according to the general rubric of a feast of the Lord with All-Night Vigil without Litiya. It has everything proper to itself, and at the Liturgy - Antiphons and Entrance Hymn. However, before the Troparion of Flowery [Sunday] we sing yesterday's Troparion of Lazarus; only after the Great Doxology we sing the Troparion of Lazarus alone.
AT Great Vespers: On "Lord, I have cried" - stichera on 10; and at the blessing of loaves - Troparion of Lazarus twice and of Flowery [Sunday] - once.
#### 4.2.2.1 AT Great Matins
##### 4.2.2.1.1 On "God is the Lord": Troparion of Lazarus twice, Glory, Both now: of Flowery [Sunday]
##### 4.2.2.1.2 The blessing and distribution of branches takes place, according to the rubric, immediately after the 50th Psalm, during the kissing of the Gospel. According to our custom, the blessing takes place after the 50th Psalm, and the distribution - during the anointing with oil (myrovanie), that is during the canon. And it takes place thus: When the Priest has sung the Gospel, he places it on the analogion near the tetrapod, between two candles lit on candlesticks. On the tetrapod are placed branches and prepared censers and sprinklers. The Priest approaches the tetrapod and at the time when the Choir reads the 50th Psalm, having put incense in and blessed the censer, censes the branches from the four sides of the tetrapod and, after the conclusion of the 50th Psalm, when the Deacon exclaims (if there be none, then the Priest himself) "Let us pray to the Lord", reads aloud (from the Sluzhebnik (lit. "Service Book") or from the Triodion[^563] ) the first prayer "O Lord our God", and the Choir responds: "Amen". Priest: "Peace be to all"; deacon: "Bow your heads unto the Lord"; and, after "To Thee, O Lord", the Priest reads the second prayer "O Lord God Almighty... unto ages of ages"[^564]; choir: "Amen"; the Priest sprinkles the branches with holy water, saying: "These branches are blessed and sanctified" and the rest; and, after singing the litany "Save, O God", with His right hand anoints the people, and with His left hand distributes to all the branches, which the Deacon solemnly hands to him, and if there be no deacon, then one of the church servers[^565]
After the 9th Ode we do not read the Exaposteilarion, but immediately: "Holy is the Lord our God" (3) in Tone 4.
AT THE LITURGY: After the Entrance - Troparion of Lazarus, Glory: of FLOWERY [SUNDAY], Both now: Kontakion of FLOWERY [SUNDAY]. We sing the Trisagion.
FAST FOR THE WHOLE PASSION WEEK
On Monday, Wednesday and Friday the Synod of Lviv prescribes a strict fast. On Tuesday, Thursday and Saturday it permits also dairy products (Title XI, part 4).
### 4.2.3 Great Monday, Tuesday and Wednesday
Notes
##### 4.2.3.1 The sacred vestments of these days and of the whole Passion Week, beginning from Flowery Sunday evening, should be of violet color
##### 4.2.3.2 All these three days have one form of divine service, that is Lenten, as in the Forty Days [Lent], only with certain exceptions, as given below:
AT VESPERS
a) On Flowery Sunday evening
##### 4.2.3.3 On "Lord, I have cried" - stichera of the Triodion on 6, Glory: Triodion, Both now: Triodion
##### 4.2.3.4 Entrance, Prokimenon of the day ("Bless the Lord now") and immediately "Vouchsafe, O Lord"
##### 4.2.3.5 Aposticha of the Triodion with specific verses
##### 4.2.3.6 After "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart" with the Trisagion - Troparion "Virgin Theotokos" and everything else with prostrations, as on the previous Sundays of Great Lent
##### 4.2.3.7 Dismissal specific, so also on Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday and Thursday at the end of the Hours: "Christ the Lord, Who goes"[^567]
b) On Monday, Tuesday and Wednesday evening
AT VESPERS WITH PRESANCTIFIED
##### 4.2.3.8 On "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: all - of the Triodion, Glory, Both now: Triodion
##### 4.2.3.9 Entrance with the Gospel and, after "O Gladsome Light", - two Prokimena of the Triodion, each - with its reading
##### 4.2.3.10 After "Let my prayer be set forth" the Priest: "And that we may be accounted worthy" and the rest and reads the Gospel of Matthew, Section 98 and the rest of the Presanctified with the dismissal "Christ the Lord, Who goes"
Note: If there be no Presanctified, then on "Lord, I have cried" we sing stichera on 6, and the three last we transfer to the Aposticha with the usual verses, singing the first twice. We make the Entrance with the Gospel and, after the second reading of the Triodion, we sing the Gospel, as above, and everything else with prostrations, according to the Lenten custom.
#### 4.2.3.1 AT COMPLINE
On Sunday evening we sing Small Compline, according to the rubric of other Sundays of Great Lent, and at it - the triodion of the Triodion, and after the Trisagion - Kontakion of the Triodion only.
On Monday and Tuesday evening - Great Compline with prostrations according to the Lenten rubric, and at it also - the triodion of the Triodion and after the Trisagion - Kontakion of the Triodion, then "O Lord of Hosts, be with us"[^568].
On Wednesday evening we sing Small Compline[^569], also at it - the triodion of the Triodion and, after the Trisagion, - Kontakion of the Triodion only.
AT THE Midnight Office: Nothing special.
AT MATINS
##### 4.2.3.1.1 At Alleluia, instead of the Trinity Hymns, we sing the Troparion "Behold the Bridegroom" twice, Glory, Both now: the same
##### 4.2.3.1.2 Three regular Kathismata with their Sessional Hymns, then "And that we may be accounted worthy", Gospel of Matins[^570], according to the sequence of Gospels, Psalm 50 and "Save, O God"[^571]
##### 4.2.3.1.3 Canon only one: triodion on Monday and Wednesday, or diodion [two-ode canon] on Tuesday. Troparia - on 12, heirmoi - twice. We do not recite the [Biblical] Odes, but only the refrain to the troparia of the canon "Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee", then the Katavasia, heirmos of the canon once both choirs together. Before the Kontakion-Ikos the Priest sings the Small Litany; at the 9th Ode we do not sing "More honorable", nor "It is truly meet" after the canon, but immediately after the Katavasia - Small Litany and Exaposteilarion "Thy bridal chamber" twice, Glory, Both now: three times the same
##### 4.2.3.1.4 Stichera of the Praises - 4: of the Triodion, Glory, Both now: Triodion, Small Doxology and, after "Let us complete", - Aposticha of the Triodion and everything else with prostrations, as on the Lenten days of Great Lent
#### 4.2.3.2 AT THE HOURS
Everything - according to the general Lenten rubric of Great Lent, only:
##### 4.2.3.2.1 At the 1st and 9th Hours there will be no Kathisma, but it will be only at the 3rd and 6th
##### 4.2.3.2.2 Immediately after the Theotokion "O Theotokos, Thou Art the vine" at the 3rd Hour and, after the corresponding Theotokia, at the other two Hours, the rubrics prescribe the reading of the Four Gospels, which today among us is read only in cathedral, or conciliar, churches[^573]. At the 6th Hour we read the Gospel not immediately after the Theotokion, but after the conclusion of the Troparion of the Prophecy and the Paremia with its Prokimena. The reading of the Four Gospels covers all the Evangelists, that is Matthew, Mark and Luke in full, and John - only up to the "Passion of Christ", that is up to "Now is the Son of man glorified", Section 46, verse 13.32. All the Evangelists, taken together, are divided into 9 readings, according to the number of the three Hours (3, 6, 9) and three days (Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday). Matthew is divided into 2 readings, Mark - into 2, John - into 2, and Luke - into 3. The first reading covers the first half of Matthew, the second - the second half of Matthew, the third - the first half of Mark, the fourth - the second half of Mark, the fifth - the first part of Luke, the sixth - the second part of Luke, the seventh - the third part of Luke, the eighth - the first half of John, the ninth - the second half of John. The rubric does not indicate where the first half of the three Evangelists and the first and second parts of Luke end. But since Matthew has 28 chapters, its half ends with the 14th chapter, Mark - 16 chapters, so its half ends with the 8th chapter, Luke - 24 chapters, so its first part ends with the 8th chapter, and the second - with the 16th. John, up to "Now is [the Son of man] glorified", has 12 chapters and part of the 13th chapter, therefore its half ends with the 6th chapter and part of the 7th
The Priest, at the beginning of each reading, dressed in a phelonion of Lenten color, opens the holy doors and censes everything as usual, then, having come out through the holy doors to the analogion placed before the holy doors facing the people, on which stand two candlesticks with lit candles, reads from there the Gospel, beginning from "And that we may be accounted worthy". After the conclusion of each reading, having kissed the beginning of the reading and leaving the Gospel on the analogion until the end of the 3rd reading, he returns to the sanctuary and, having closed the holy doors, takes off the phelonion[^574].
##### 4.2.3.2.3 After the Trisagion - Kontakion of the Triodion
AT THE TYPIKA
##### 4.2.3.2.4 Only one Kontakion of the Triodion
##### 4.2.3.2.5 In Slavonic rubrics and the Greek Typikon of Sabbas, on Great Wednesday, instead of the dismissal the prayer "O Master most merciful" is given, which is at Vespers with All-Night Vigil, and in Slavonic ones - also the prayer of forgiveness for sins committed in Great Lent, and commemorations. However, these superfluous additions of the Greco-Slavs are unknown to the Greeks. The prayer "O Master most merciful" and the forgiveness may be retained perhaps only in monasteries. The commemorations, which are superfluous here, will be taken later, at the end of Compline and the Midnight Office, which have nothing in common with forgiveness for the sins of the Forty Days
On Wednesday
AT VESPERS WITH PRESANCTIFIED,
AT COMPLINE AND AT THE Midnight Office
Everything - according to the rubric of Monday and Tuesday, here on pp. 379-380 [→REF:p379-380], only on Wednesday, after the dismissal of the Liturgy of the Presanctified, the prostrations in church cease, and in the cells are made until the ringing for Matins of the bright Resurrection of Christ[^575]. We, however, until the Resurrection of Christ make three great prostrations in church, at the end of the Divine Service, singing the verse "Though Thou didst suffer", and during the veneration of the Shroud, singing "The noble Joseph".

--- Match 23 [Terms: according to our custom] ---
### 4.2.8 Resurrection Matins
##### 4.2.8.1 PROCESSION BEFORE THE CHURCH DOORS. All people go out of the church, and after them go out also the sacred ministers. And the procession takes place thus: All sacred ministers, in all their sacred bright vestments, stand before the steps of the altar. The main sacred minister takes the cross, the Deacon - the censer, all together bow low to the Holy Mysteries and turn to the west. The main one begins to sing the sticheron "Thy Resurrection, O Christ Savior", all take up the singing of the sticheron and go out from the sanctuary to the church: the Priest - through the holy [doors], and all other sacred ministers - through the Deacon's doors; at this time all bells ring, and all go to the great church doors and, having gone out of the church, stand according to rank before the church doors, which are immediately closed[^615]. In the middle of the church is placed on the holy table first of all the icon of the Resurrection of Christ, usually on the Artos, also other holy icons, reliquaries and the book of the Holy Gospels in the most precious binding or something else of the sacred things, all this - according to the number of priests who will give them to be kissed by the people at the end of Matins. Outside, before the church doors, Matins begins in the following way[^616]
##### 4.2.8.2 BEGINNING OF MATINS. Immediately after the church doors are closed, the main priest, holding with his left hand the cross, and with his right - the censer and censing the church doors[^617], exclaims loudly: "Glory to the Holy" and the rest. The Choir responds: "Amen"; the Priest gives up the censer and sings loudly the troparion "Christ is risen", also all - the same, then the Priest the second time - the same and all - the same, and the third time the Priest - the same and all - the same, then the Priest in a high voice exclaims the verses, and all after each verse sing this troparion once[^618]. At the end the Priest, having sung in a higher voice the same troparion only up to "trampling down death by death" inclusive, opens the doors, and all finish: "and upon those in the tombs bestowing life" and again all bells ring, all enter the church[^619]. The Matins prayers at Resurrection Matins throughout the whole Bright Week are not recited, for they are connected with the Six Psalms, and since there will be no Six Psalms here, there will be no prayers. If it were necessary to recite the prayers, it should be indicated in the rubric where they should be recited, for during the verses "Let God arise", which take the place of the Six Psalms, it is not possible to recite them, for at this time the rubric orders the Priest to sing verses, and not to add prayers as well
##### 4.2.8.3 ENTRANCE INTO THE CHURCH. The entrance of the procession into the church takes place in the reverse order to the exit from the church, that is first enters the main priest, after him - other priests and sacred ministers, all the clergy, then singers, brotherhood, banner- and icon-bearers, the cross-bearer and all people. The priests enter the sanctuary and approach the holy table. The Bishop, if there be one, departs to his throne. The Deacon stands in his place before the holy doors and sings from there the litany
##### 4.2.8.4 After the entrance into the church the first deacon sings the Great Litany "In peace let us pray to the Lord", standing in the usual place, and immediately we sing the Canon
##### 4.2.8.5 Canon of the Resurrection: heirmoi - 4 times (in Greek ones - twice), troparia - on 12. (Today heirmoi - once, troparia with heirmos - on 4). Katavasia after each ode - heirmos of the canon, both choirs together - once. The Priest sings the heirmoi, and the choirs - the troparia and Katavasia. Refrain to the troparia of the canon - "Christ is risen from the dead", at the penultimate - "Glory", and at the last - "Both now", as usual
At the beginning of the canon, after the singing of the heirmos, the Priest censes everything, as usual[^620].
After each ode the deacons, from their usual places before the holy doors, sing the Small Litany with separate exclamations, which the priests sing alternately in the sanctuary[^621].
After the 3rd Ode - Hypakoe; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos and "The Resurrection of Christ" (3), "Jesus having risen from the grave" once (according to others - three times); at the 9th, after "O Theotokos and Mother of Light", the usual censing takes place and, instead of "More honorable" we sing "The angel cried" and "Shine, shine"; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Resurrection three times.
##### 4.2.8.6 Stichera of the Praises - 8: 4 Sunday of the Octoechos of Tone 1, with the usual verses from "Praise Him", and 4 of the Resurrection with specific verses, then Glory, Both now: to the Resurrection, "Christ is risen" (3), can be more, until the kissing is finished
##### 4.2.8.7 KISSING ONE ANOTHER. Is prescribed in the rubrics at "Christ is risen", after the conclusion of the last sticheron of the Resurrection. However, today we do not kiss one another as in the sticheron: "and let us embrace one another", but we kiss only the precious cross, the Gospel and holy icons, which the priests hold, greeting everyone who approaches with the words: "Christ is risen", and they answer: "Truly He is risen". If there be many people, then we begin the kissing not after the stichera of the Resurrection, but from their beginning, that is from the verse "Let God arise". If there be very many people, then the kissing will be also during the canon. And this kissing takes place thus:
If there be only one priest, he stands in the holy doors and from there gives the cross to be kissed by the people, greeting everyone: "Christ is risen", and the one Who kisses answers: "Truly He is risen". If there be several priests, then first the main one goes out from the holy doors and stands before the iconostasis, on the right side, that is opposite the icon of the Savior, looking to the north. After Him the second priest comes out from the holy doors with the Gospel, approaches the main one, and he gives him the cross to kiss, saying: "Christ is risen", having kissed, in His turn, gives Him the Gospel to kiss, answering: "Truly He is risen" and stands on His left. Then comes out the third priest with the icon of the Resurrection of Christ, approaches the first and He gives Him the cross to kiss, saying: "Christ is risen", and having kissed gives Him the icon of the Resurrection to kiss, answering: "Truly He is risen", and approaches the second priest, who gives him the Gospel to kiss, saying: "Christ is risen", and He, having kissed the Gospel, gives the second to kiss the icon of the Resurrection, answering: "Truly He is risen", and stands on the left of the second. The fourth priest comes out with the icon of the Theotokos, approaches the first, also the second and the third, of whom each gives him his object to kiss, saying: "Christ is risen", and having kissed gives each of them the icon of the Theotokos to kiss, answering: "Truly He is risen". So do also all other priests, as many as there be. In cathedral churches the Bishop, sitting on his throne opposite the icon of the Savior, gives the cross to be kissed, also each of the canons gives His insignia to be kissed, sitting on the seat. And when the kissing between the priests ends, then other sacred ministers and all the people approach one by one each Priest in turn, starting from the first, and kiss his insignia, answering: "Truly He is risen"[^622]. After the kissing once was read the "Homily" of Chrysostom, which is in the Flower Triodion.
##### 4.2.8.8 Litanies "Have mercy on us, O God" and "Let us complete" are sung by deacons, standing in their usual places
##### 4.2.8.9 Great Dismissal, specific, at which, instead of "More honorable", we sing "Shine, shine"[^638] and, instead of "Glory, Both now", we sing "Christ is risen" once. The Priest stands with the cross in the holy doors, and the Deacon (if there be none, then the Priest himself) exclaims: "Wisdom". Choir (or second deacon, if there be one)[^623]: "Bless"
Priest: "Blessed be Christ our God... ages".
Choir: "Amen". "Confirm, O God, the catholic faith unto ages of ages".
Priest: "Most Holy Theotokos, save us".
Choir: "Shine, shine...".
Priest: "Glory to Thee, O Christ God, our hope, glory to Thee".
Choir: "Christ is risen from the dead... bestowing". "Lord, have mercy" (3), "Lord, bless".
Priest: Dismissal with the cross: "Christ, Who rose from the dead, trampling down death by death and upon those in the tombs bestowing life, our true God, through the prayers of His most pure Mother, of the holy, glorious and all-laudable apostles, of our venerable and God-bearing fathers and of all the saints, have mercy on us and save us, for He is good and loves mankind".
Then, raising the cross up, exclaims: "Christ is risen" (3), and we each time answer: "Truly He is risen". We sing the final "Christ is risen" (3), the whole troparion, and after this we finish with the singing "and to us He has granted life eternal, let us worship His Resurrection on the third day"[^624].
Note on the doors of the iconostasis
"Let it be known also that throughout the whole Bright Week the great holy doors of the iconostasis and the small side ones in all churches remain open also during communion" (Rubric of our Pochaiv Triodion and Russian Typika).
RESURRECTION HOURS
Instead of each of the four Hours, also instead of Compline and the Midnight Office, throughout the whole Bright Week we celebrate a short order, given in the Triodion, in our Horologia and Molitvoslovs at Resurrection Matins.
Note: If the Hours are read together, then "Blessed is our God" we say only at the 1st Hour, and the others we begin from "Christ is risen" (3).
#### 4.2.8.1 AT THE LITURGY
Everything - according to the general rubric of a feast of the Lord, only:
##### 4.2.8.1.1 Instead of "O Heavenly King", at the Proskomedia, at the Liturgy and wherever else, we recite "Christ is risen" (3) until the Ascension; and from the Ascension to the Descent [of the Holy Spirit], instead of "O Heavenly King", we read nothing[^625]
##### 4.2.8.1.2 Regarding "Christ is risen", one should know that at "Glory to Thee, O Christ God" before the dismissal of the Proskomedia and at the Liturgy we recite "Christ is risen" once, "Lord, have mercy" (3) and "Lord, bless"
At "Blessed is the Kingdom" we sing "Christ is risen" three times, that is once - the Priest and both choirs - once each (although in our Triodion is given: "priest - three times and choirs the same - three times"), and where there is only one choir, then the Priest - once and the Choir - once, then half - the Priest and half (from "and upon those in the tombs") - the Choir.
Also at "With the fear of God", instead of "Blessed is He that cometh" and instead of "We have seen the true light", we sing "Christ is risen" once. Instead of "Let our mouths be filled" and "Blessed be the name of the Lord" - "Christ is risen" (3)[^626]. Among us usually "Christ is risen" is recited also instead of "Be exalted, O God, above the heavens", however, this violates the memory of the mystery of Christ's Ascension, which is mentioned in this place of the Liturgy from the beginning. Our priests say "Christ is risen" (only these two words) also after "With the fear of God", after "Save, O God" and after "Always, now and ever", however, in no rubric do we find this.
##### 4.2.8.1.3 Instead of the Trisagion we sing "All Who have been baptized into Christ"
##### 4.2.8.1.4 During the reading of the Gospel, at each verse, all bells are rung[^627]
Our Sluzhebnyky: "If served conciliarly, then this Gospel is read by all priests and deacons by verses as the Superior decides". About this you have a detailed rubric in our and Russian Triodia. However, the custom that one and the same Gospel be read by all priests and deacons - is a superfluous invention of the Greco-Slavs, which today takes place among us only when the Gospel is read in different languages, as happens in our cathedral temples, in which the Bishop reads it in Greek from the holy doors, then the Deacon - in Slavonic from the ambo, and at the end canons and priests, placed throughout the whole church, in different liturgical languages. In Russia the Bishop reads the Gospel in Slavonic, and other priests - in Hebrew, in Greek and in Latin, in those languages in which the inscription on the Cross was written, then in other new languages (Muralt. 14. Brief. S. 142). The Greeks also read the Gospel, according to the new custom, in different languages, however, not at the Liturgy, but at Vespers (the Gospel read at Vespers of this day), as happens in the Patriarchate of Constantinople[^628].
##### 4.2.8.1.5 Instead of "It is truly meet", according to the new rubric, we sing the heirmos "Shine, shine" with the refrain "The angel cried" as in the Triodion and in the Constantinopolitan Typikon, but without the troparia of the 9th Ode of the Canon, as in our Sluzhebnyky, for these troparia are not about the Most Holy Theotokos, but about Christ's Resurrection
##### 4.2.8.1.6 Before the dismissal, instead of "More honorable", we sing "Shine, shine" and instead of "Glory, Both now" - "Christ is risen" once. Dismissal with the cross and the rest, which is after the dismissal with the raising of the cross exactly as was indicated for Resurrection Matins
##### 4.2.8.1.7 BLESSING OF THE ARTOS. After the Prayer behind the Ambo[^629] takes place the blessing of the Artos, for which you have the prayer in the Sluzhebnik after the Liturgy of the Resurrection; after the prayer the Artos is sprinkled with holy water. And this Artos with the feast, that is the icon of the Resurrection, after the dismissal of the Liturgy, is carried from the altar, on which it was blessed, to the tetrapod, where it remains until Bright Saturday and the faithful entering the church kiss it. On Bright Saturday at the Liturgy, after the Prayer behind the Ambo, the Priest, having come out through the holy doors, reads over it the prayer for breaking, given only in our Sluzhebnik on Bright Saturday, and after this, it is taken from the tetrapod, broken in the sacristy and on the next day, that is on the Sunday of Ap. Thomas, after the Prayer behind the Ambo, is distributed to the faithful by the Priest standing in the holy doors[^630]
Note: Throughout the whole Bright Week it is permitted to eat meat dishes.
BLESSING OF PASCHAL FOODS
It takes place, according to our custom and according to the rubric of the Triodion, outside the church or after the Liturgy (and then, going out of the church, we sing the Stichera of the Resurrection at will), or after Matins, or in homes on Saturday evening. The order of this blessing with sprinkling with holy water at the end you have both in the Sluzhebnik, and in the Trebnik, and in the Triodion, and in the Molitvoslov, after the Resurrection Hours.
### 4.2.9 On the Day of Resurrection in the Evening
AT VESPERS
Notes
##### 4.2.9.1 The sacred ministers, dressed in all sacred vestments of their rank during the whole Vespers stand in the sanctuary at the altar, coming out only for the Entrance (and deacons - also for the litanies) before the holy doors and for the final kissing of the sacred insignia, if there be several priests - as was given for Matins[^631]
##### 4.2.9.2 Censing is done three times, that is at the usual beginning, only before the holy table, then at "Lord, I have cried" and at "O Gladsome Light", as usual
##### 4.2.9.3 Entrance takes place with the Gospel, for it is to be read
RUBRIC
##### 4.2.9.4 The Priest and deacons, if there be any, dressed in all sacred vestments of their rank, come before the steps of the altar and bow low, as usual. The Priest receives the censer with the usual prayer of blessing the censer, censes before the holy table, exclaiming: "Blessed is our God"[^632]; then, having given up the censer, ascends with the deacons the steps and sings "Christ is risen" once and both choirs - once each. After this the Priest recites clearly "Let God arise" and other verses with "Glory, Both now". The choirs, at each verse, sing "Christ is risen" once or three times exactly as was indicated for the beginning of Matins. Afterwards the Priest: "Christ is risen" up to "trampling down death by death", and the choirs: "and upon those in the tombs" and the rest
2.	"In peace let us pray to the Lord" the Priest sings at the altar, and if a Deacon - then before the iconostasis.
3.	"Lord, I have cried" - in Tone 2, with the usual censing. Stichera - Resurrection of the Octoechos of Tone 2, given in the Triodion; Glory: Triodion, Both now: 1st Theotokion (Dogmatikon) of Tone 2.
##### 4.2.9.5 Entrance with the Gospel and usual censing at "O Gladsome Light"; and, after the Great Prokimenon, the Deacon, and if there be none, then the Priest himself: "And that we may be accounted worthy", priest: "The Reading from John...". Deacon: "Let us attend", and the Priest himself reads the Gospel from the holy doors (From John Sect. 65, up to "not believe")
5.	"Let us all say", "Vouchsafe" and "Let us complete".
##### 4.2.9.6 At the Aposticha: one Resurrection Sticheron of the Octoechos, given in the Triodion, then Resurrection Stichera with their verses and the kissing of the cross, or also of other sacred insignia exactly as was given for Matins. After the final "Christ is risen" immediately dismissal with the cross and the rest exactly as was given for Matins
COMPLINE AND Midnight Office: Everything - according to the pattern of the Resurrection Hours, here on page 416.
### 4.2.10 General Rubric for All Days of Bright Week
AT VESPERS
Everything - as on the day of Resurrection in the evening, here on p. 421 [→REF:p421], except the Entrance, which takes place with the censer, and not with the Gospel, and there will be no Gospel.
Note
According to the previous rubric the Priest, at Vespers of Bright Week, from Monday onwards, should not use all sacred vestments, but from the beginning - only the epitrachelion, from the Entrance to the end - also the phelonion[^633]. Among us the custom is observed to use all sacred vestments on the first three days of Bright Week, that is on Sunday, Monday and Tuesday evening, since our people mark all these three days by abstention from work and presence at divine service. On other days of this week it is sufficient to use only the epitrachelion and phelonion.
COMPLINE AND Midnight Office: According to the pattern of the Resurrection Hours, here on page 416.
AT MATINS
Everything - according to the rubric of the Resurrection, here on page 408, only:
##### 4.2.10.1 At the beginning of Matins we do not go out before the church doors, but begin it in the sanctuary, before the altar[^634]
##### 4.2.10.2 We sing the Small Litany only after the 3rd, 6th and 9th Odes
#### 4.2.10.1 AT THE HOURS
Exactly as on the day of Resurrection, only on Bright Saturday the 9th Hour is read already with three psalms, as usual, and at it - Resurrection Troparion of Tone 8 "From the height", and Kontakion - to the Resurrection "Though Thou didst descend into the grave".
#### 4.2.10.2 AT THE LITURGY
##### 4.2.10.2.1 Everything - as on the day of Resurrection, changing daily the Prokimenon, Apostle, Alleluia and Gospel
##### 4.2.10.2.2 On Saturday, after the Prayer behind the Ambo, we read the prayer for the breaking of the Artos, given in our Liturgikon, in the order of Bright Saturday[^635]
GENERAL RUBRICS from the Sunday of Ap. Thomas to the Ascension
To the divine service from the Sunday of Ap. Thomas to the Ascension one could apply the general rule in the middle of a feast of the Lord. However, since each Sunday, beginning from the Sunday of Ap. Thomas, has a separate name and a separate service, which extends to the other weekdays of the given week and is as if a separate feast in the Easter time, the place of the Resurrection service is taken by the service of the Sunday, also on weekdays, together with some parts of the Sunday service of the Octoechos, except the eight-day feast of Mid-Pentecost, to which the Sunday service yields on weekdays. General rules of this time are as follows:
##### 4.2.10.2.3 At the beginning of every service, instead of "O Heavenly King", we sing "Christ is risen" (3)[^636], and at the end of every divine service, according to our custom, we also sing "Christ is risen" (3), also at the end we add: "and to us He has granted life eternal, let us worship His Resurrection on the third day"[^637]; and before the dismissal, instead of "More honorable", we sing "Shine, shine"[^638]
##### 4.2.10.2.4 At Vespers: On Sunday evening for Monday there will be no Entrance, as happens on the Sundays of Lent (for here there is no Great Prokimenon, and the Triodia give the litany "Have mercy on us, O God" at the end of Vespers), except the Sunday of Ap. Thomas, which, as a feast of the Lord, has also a Great Prokimenon. Finally, in the provinces the Great Entrance is made every Sunday evening, regardless of whether there is a feast or not
##### 4.2.10.2.5 At Compline: From today we begin to sing the Canons of the Theotokos. After "It is truly meet" throughout the whole week, we recite the Kontakion of the Sunday; only during the eight days of the feast of Mid-Pentecost we read the Kontakion of Mid-Pentecost
##### 4.2.10.2.6 We sing the Midnight Office as usual, only after the 1st Trisagion on the feast of Mid-Pentecost[^642] we take the Troparion of Mid-Pentecost, and on the Apodosis of the Resurrection[^643] - the Resurrection Troparion of the Sunday with its Theotokion; and after the 2nd Trisagion in the middle of Mid-Pentecost we take the Kontakion of Mid-Pentecost, and outside Mid-Pentecost - Kontakion of the Resurrection. The prayer "Remember, O Lord" will not be
##### 4.2.10.2.7 We begin Matins from "Glory to the Holy" and immediately "Christ is risen" (3), then "Glory to God in the highest" and the rest; before the 50th Psalm we recite "The Resurrection of Christ": on Sundays - three times, and on weekdays - once[^639]
##### 4.2.10.2.8 At the Hours: after "Blessed is our God" we recite "Christ is risen" (3), and after the dismissal - "Christ is risen" (3) and add: "and to us He has granted life eternal" and further
Note: Since we do not exclaim "Blessed is our God" when the Hour is combined with Matins or with the preceding one, also since we do not impart the dismissal when the Hour is combined with the following one, then we do not read "Christ is risen" then.
##### 4.2.10.2.9 At the Liturgy: "Christ is risen" we sing where also on the Resurrection of Christ; here, on pp. 416-418 [→REF:p416-418]
Antiphons: the two first - Resurrection, and the third - daily with the Resurrection refrain, also at "O come, let us worship" both on Sundays and on weekdays.
We conclude the troparia with the Kontakion of the Resurrection, except the Week of Thomas and Mid-Pentecost, for then we conclude with their Kontakion just as in the Triodion; although the troparia of the Week of Thomas can be concluded also with the Kontakion of the Resurrection, as it is in our Sluzhebnyky, in our Manuscript Typikon and Tserkovne Oko.
Instead of "It is truly meet" we sing "The angel cried" and "Shine, shine", except the eight days of Mid-Pentecost, which has its heirmos.
We sing the Resurrection dismissal, except the Week of Thomas, which has a specific dismissal. The dismissal with the cross we sing only on Sundays[^641], on the feast of Mid-Pentecost and on the Apodosis of the Resurrection.
We suppose that also on some other days, when there be a sufficient number of people, it would be good to sing the dismissal with the cross, however, not every day, as some do, having for this neither rubric nor general custom.
##### 4.2.10.2.10 The services of saints without Polyeleos, which fall on these Sundays, as also on others until Pentecost, on the feast of Mid-Pentecost and on the Apodosis of the Resurrection, we take at Compline
SPECIFIC RUBRICS
from the Sunday of Ap. Thomas to the Ascension
### 4.2.11 Sunday of the Apostle Thomas
Second Sunday from the Resurrection. Tone 1.
We serve All-Night Vigil.
We sing everything according to the general rubric of a feast of the Lord of the first rank, however, at Great Vespers there will be no readings, at Matins we sing "The Resurrection of Christ" (3) (according to others - once), and at the Liturgy there will be no specific antiphons; the feast is leavetaken on the following Saturday.
WEEKDAYS OF THE WEEK OF AP. THOMAS
On the weekdays until the Sunday of the Myrrh-bearers we sing everything according to the general rubric in the middle of a feast; only at the Aposticha of Vespers and at the Sessional Hymns of Matins, which are after the 1st Stichology, and at the Praises (for there will be stichera also at the Praises and afterwards, at the Aposticha) there will be some stichera taken from the weekday service of the Octoechos, according to the tone and day of the week, except Saturday, which has the full service of the feast. Also at Matins, before the 50th Psalm - "The Resurrection of Christ" once. On Saturday we sing 3 canons: of the feast with heirmoi on 6, of the saint whose temple it is on 4 and of the Menaion on 4.

--- Match 24 [Terms: according to our custom] ---
### 4.2.12 Sunday of the Myrrh-Bearing Women
Third from the Resurrection. Tone 2.
#### 4.2.12.1 AT Great Vespers
1.	"Blessed is our God", "Christ is risen" (3), Trisagion and the rest with "Our Father", "Lord, have mercy" (12), "O come, let us worship" (3).
##### 4.2.12.1.1 On "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 4 Sunday and 6 of the Myrrh-bearers (3 which are at "Lord, I have cried", and 3 which are at the Litiya); Glory: of the Myrrh-bearers, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone (Dogmatikon)
##### 4.2.12.1.2 At the Aposticha - Stichera of the Resurrection with their verses; Glory: "Thee, Who Art clothed with light", Both now: "The Day of Resurrection"[^644]
##### 4.2.12.1.3 After the final Trisagion: Sunday Troparion of Tone 2; Glory: "The noble Joseph", Both now: "The angel stood by the tomb"
##### 4.2.12.1.4 Dismissal of the Resurrection, without the cross
AT COMPLINE: After "It is truly meet" - Kontakion of the Myrrh-bearers.
#### 4.2.12.2 AT Great Matins
##### 4.2.12.2.1 On "God is the Lord": Sunday Troparion twice, Glory: "The noble Joseph", Both now: "The angel stood by the tomb"
##### 4.2.12.2.2 After the 1st and 2nd Stichology - Sessional Hymns of the Myrrh-bearers; after "The Blameless" - "The Choir of angels"; Hypakoe of the tone and everything else up to the canon - Sunday, as usual. Gospel will be the 2nd Sunday and "The Resurrection of Christ" three times (according to others - once)
##### 4.2.12.2.3 Canons 3 [making] 14: Resurrection with heirmos on 4, Theotokos of the Triodion on 2 and of the Myrrh-bearers on 8. Katavasia of the Resurrection
After the 3rd Ode - Sessional Hymn of the Triodion[^645]; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Triodion; at the 9th we do not sing "More honorable"[^646], but we sing "The angel cried" and "Shine, shine"; after the 9th - "Holy is the Lord our God" (3) and Exaposteilarion of the Resurrection, Glory: of the Myrrh-bearers, Both now: the same.
##### 4.2.12.2.4 From the Praises to the end everything - according to the general rubric of the Sunday
AT THE HOURS: At all - Sunday Troparion, Glory: "The noble Joseph"; Kontakion - of the Myrrh-bearers.
AT THE LITURGY: After the Entrance - Troparion Sunday and "The noble Joseph"; Glory: Kontakion of the Triodion, Both now: of the Resurrection ("Though Thou didst descend into the grave").
WEEKDAYS OF THE WEEK OF THE MYRRH-BEARERS
Everything - according to the general rubric of a saint in the middle of a feast; only at the Aposticha of Vespers and at the Sessional Hymns, which are after the 1st Stichology of Matins and at the Praises (for there will be stichera also at the Praises and afterwards, at the Aposticha) there will be some stichera or troparia, taken from the weekday service of the Octoechos of the current tone, different on different weekdays, except Saturday, which has everything of the feast. And in particular, at the end of Vespers: Troparion "The noble Joseph", Glory: of the saint, Both now: to the Myrrh-bearers, the Troparia "The noble Joseph" and "When Thou didst descend" in the evening we take alternately, every other day[^647].
AT COMPLINE: After the Trisagion - Kontakion of the Sunday. "Thus we take until the Sunday of All Saints" (Triodion).
AT MATINS
On "God is the Lord": Troparion "The noble Joseph" once and "When Thou didst descend" once, Glory: of the saint, Both now: to the Myrrh-bearers. If there be two saints with their troparia, then, we suppose, the Sunday one need not be taken.
At the Sessional Hymns and at the Praises there will be two troparia or two weekday stichera of the Octoechos of the current tone, with the usual refrains; before the 50th Psalm - "The Resurrection of Christ" once, at the end of Matins the troparia just as on "God is the Lord".
#### 4.2.12.3 AT THE HOURS
Since no rubric gives anything about this, we consider that it should be thus: At the 1st - Troparion "The noble Joseph", at the 3rd - Sunday and Glory: of the saint, at the 6th - to the Myrrh-bearers, at the 9th - again "The noble Joseph" and Glory: of the saint. Kontakia: at the 1st and 6th - of the Myrrh-bearers, at the 3rd and 9th - of the saint. If there be two saints, then at the 3rd, after "Glory", will be the Troparion to the first, and at the 9th - to the second saint[^648].
#### 4.2.12.4 AT THE LITURGY
Troparia: "The noble Joseph", Sunday and to the Myrrh-bearers; Glory: Kontakion of the Myrrh-bearers, Both now: Kontakion of the Resurrection[^649]; or Glory, Both now: Kontakion of the Myrrh-bearers, without the Kontakion of the Resurrection[^650].
If the service to a saint or for some need is also taken, then after the three troparia of the Sunday we take also the troparion to the saint or for the need; also the Kontakion to the Myrrh-bearers, Glory: Kontakion to the saint or for the need, and Both now: Kontakion to the Myrrh-bearers. Epistle and Gospel of the day. Everything else - of the Sunday of the Myrrh-bearers or also to the saint or for the need, if there be any.
### 4.2.13 Sunday of the Paralytic
Fourth from the Resurrection. Tone 3.
#### 4.2.13.1 AT Great Vespers
##### 4.2.13.1.1 On "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 7 of the Octoechos (given also in the Triodion) and of the Triodion on 3. Glory: Triodion, Both now: Dogmatikon of the tone
##### 4.2.13.1.2 At the Aposticha one Sunday sticheron, then Stichera of the Resurrection, Glory: of the Paralytic, Both now: "The Day of Resurrection"
##### 4.2.13.1.3 After "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart" - Sunday Troparion of Tone 3, Glory, Both now: Sunday Theotokion in the tone of the Troparion
##### 4.2.13.1.4 Dismissal of the Resurrection, without the cross
AT COMPLINE: After "It is truly meet" - Kontakion of the Paralytic only.
#### 4.2.13.2 AT Great Matins
##### 4.2.13.2.1 On "God is the Lord": Sunday Troparion twice, Glory, Both now: Sunday Theotokion in the tone of the Troparion
##### 4.2.13.2.2 Two usual Kathismata, Sunday Sessional Hymns, "The Blameless", everything else up to the canon - Sunday, as usual, only Gospel - 4th Sunday and "The Resurrection of Christ" (3)
##### 4.2.13.2.3 Canons 3 [making] 14: Resurrection with heirmos on 6, Theotokos of the Triodion on 2 and of the Paralytic on 6. Katavasia of the Resurrection
After the 3rd Ode - Sessional Hymn of the Paralytic, after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Paralytic; at the 9th we do not sing "More honorable", but we sing "The angel cried" and "Shine, shine"; after the 9th - "Holy is the Lord our God" (3) and Exaposteilarion of the Resurrection once, Glory: of the Paralytic, Both now: the same.
##### 4.2.13.2.4 Stichera of the Praises - 8 Sunday, as usual; Glory: of the Paralytic, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou"
##### 4.2.13.2.5 After the Great Doxology - Sunday Troparion, one of the two
##### 4.2.13.2.6 Dismissal of the Resurrection; after the dismissal - 3rd Gospel Sticheron
AT THE HOURS: At all - Sunday Troparion and Kontakion of the Paralytic.
#### 4.2.13.3 AT THE LITURGY
After the Entrance: Sunday Troparion, Glory: Kontakion of the Paralytic, Both now: Kontakion of the Resurrection. Everything else - of the Paralytic. Communion Hymn - "Receive the Body of Christ" and "Praise the Lord".
WEEKDAYS OF THE WEEK OF THE PARALYTIC
The service of the Paralytic covers only two weekdays - Monday and Tuesday, for on Wednesday the feast of Mid-Pentecost already begins.
On these two weekdays we sing everything according to the rubric of the weekdays of the Week of the Myrrh-bearers here, on page 429, only the troparia we take thus: at the end of Vespers and Matins - Sunday Troparion, Glory: of the saint, Both now: Sunday Theotokion in the tone of the Troparion of the Saint[^651]. On "God is the Lord": Sunday Troparion twice, Glory: of the saint, Both now: Sunday Theotokion in the tone of the Troparion of the Saint.
At all Hours: Sunday Troparion, and at the 3rd and 9th - also Glory: of the saint. Kontakia: at the 1st and 6th - of the Paralytic, at the 3rd and 9th - of the saint.
At the Liturgy: Sunday Troparion, Glory: Kontakion of the Paralytic, Both now: Kontakion of the Resurrection. Epistle and Gospel of the day, everything else - of the Sunday. When the service to a saint or for a need is also taken, then: Sunday Troparion and of the saint or for the need; then - Kontakion of the Paralytic, Glory: of the saint or for the need, Both now: Kontakion of the Resurrection. Everything else - as above, and also to the saint or for the need; only Communion Hymn - "Receive the Body of Christ" and to the saint or for the need.
Note: If two saints fall, then at Vespers or at Matins we select the Theotokion to the troparia according to the tone of the second, and on "God is the Lord" we sing all troparia once.
On Wednesday of the fourth week from the Resurrection
### 4.2.14 Mid-Pentecost
Note
This feast is of the Lord, of eight days, which is commemorated on the Wednesday of the Paralytic and ends on the Wednesday of the Samaritan Woman, on which it is leavetaken. On the feast itself and on the Apodosis only the service of the feast is sung, and the service of the regular saint of the Menaion is sung at Compline; on other days - it is combined with the Menaion, except the Sunday of the Samaritan Woman, for then it is combined with the service of the Sunday. This feast has no All-Night Vigil, nor Polyeleos at Matins, only the Great Doxology, and at Vespers - both Entrance and readings. We begin its service on Tuesday evening, for the liturgical day begins in the evening of the preceding day.
RUBRIC
AT VESPERS
##### 4.2.14.1 Kathisma proper
##### 4.2.14.2 On "Lord, I have cried" - stichera of the feast on 6
##### 4.2.14.3 After the Entrance - Prokimenon of the day and 3 readings of the feast
##### 4.2.14.4 Aposticha of the feast, Troparion of the feast and Great Dismissal, of Pascha
AT COMPLINE: We take the stichera and canon of the regular saint; after the Trisagion - Kontakion of the feast.
AT THE Midnight Office
After the 1st Trisagion - Troparion of the feast, and after the 2nd - Kontakion of the feast; then "Lord, have mercy" (12) and dismissal. The prayer for the dead "Remember, O Lord" we do not take.
AT MATINS
##### 4.2.14.5 On "God is the Lord": Troparion of the feast twice, Glory, Both now: the same
##### 4.2.14.6 Two regular Kathismata with the Sessional Hymn of the feast; then "The Resurrection of Christ" once, Psalm 50 and immediately - Canon
##### 4.2.14.7 Canons of the feast - 2 [making] 14: 1st - with heirmos on 8, and 2nd - on 6. Katavasia - Heirmos of the 2nd Canon
After the 3rd Ode - Sessional Hymn, after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos, after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the feast; at the 9th, instead of "More honorable", we sing the heirmos of the feast.
##### 4.2.14.8 Stichera of the Praises of the feast on 4; after the Great Doxology - Troparion of the feast, Great Dismissal, of Pascha
AT THE HOURS: At all - Troparion and Kontakion of the feast.
#### 4.2.14.1 AT THE LITURGY
Everything - to the feast, only the two first Antiphons - Resurrection, and the third - usual with the Resurrection refrain; also at "O come, let us worship". Instead of "The angel cried" and "Shine, shine" we sing the heirmos of the feast.
IN THE MIDDLE OF THE FEAST OF ### 4.2.14 Mid-Pentecost
except the Sunday of the Samaritan Woman and the Apodosis
We sing everything according to the rubric of the feast of the Sunday of Thomas here, on page 424; regarding troparia, we take them thus:
##### 4.2.14.1.1 At the end of Vespers and Matins: Troparion of the saint, Glory, Both now: Mid-Pentecost
##### 4.2.14.1.2 At Compline: Kontakion of Mid-Pentecost only
##### 4.2.14.1.3 On "God is the Lord": Troparion of Mid-Pentecost twice, Glory: of the saint, Both now: Mid-Pentecost
##### 4.2.14.1.4 At all Hours: Troparion of Mid-Pentecost, and at the 3rd and 9th - Glory: of the saint; Kontakia: at the 1st and at the 6th - to Mid-Pentecost, and at the 3rd and 9th - to the saint
##### 4.2.14.1.5 At the Liturgy: Troparion, Glory, Both now: Kontakion of Mid-Pentecost
### 4.2.15 Sunday of the Samaritan Woman
Fifth from the Resurrection. Tone 4.
Note
The Feast of Mid-Pentecost blocks the service of the Samaritan Woman on Monday, Tuesday and Wednesday of this week, and on the other three days of it, that is after the Apodosis of the feast, the service of the Samaritan Woman is taken again; on this Sunday we sing the service of the Samaritan Woman with the service of Mid-Pentecost.
RUBRIC
#### 4.2.15.1 AT Great Vespers
##### 4.2.15.1.1 On "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 4 Sunday, 3 of Mid-Pentecost and 3 of the Samaritan Woman, Glory: of the Samaritan Woman, Both now: 1st Theotokion of the tone (Dogmatikon)
##### 4.2.15.1.2 At the Aposticha: one Sunday sticheron, then Stichera of Pascha with their verses, only Doxastikon - of the Samaritan Woman
##### 4.2.15.1.3 After "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart" - Sunday Troparion, Glory, Both now: Mid-Pentecost
##### 4.2.15.1.4 Dismissal of Pascha, without the cross
AT COMPLINE: After "It is truly meet" - Kontakion of the Samaritan Woman, Glory, Both now: Kontakion of Mid-Pentecost.
#### 4.2.15.2 AT Great Matins
##### 4.2.15.2.1 On "God is the Lord": Sunday Troparion twice, Glory, Both now: Mid-Pentecost
##### 4.2.15.2.2 Kathismata proper and everything else up to the canon - Sunday, as usual, only Gospel - 7th Sunday and "The Resurrection of Christ" (3)
##### 4.2.15.2.3 Canons 4 [making] 14: Sunday with heirmos on 4, Theotokos of the Triodion on 2, Mid-Pentecost on 4 and of the Samaritan Woman on 4. Katavasia of the Resurrection
After the 3rd Ode - Kontakion-Ikos of Mid-Pentecost and Sessional Hymn of the Samaritan Woman, Glory, Both now: Mid-Pentecost. After the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Samaritan Woman. At the 9th, instead of "More honorable", - "The angel cried" and "Shine, shine"; after the 9th - "Holy is the Lord our God" (3) and Exaposteilarion of the Resurrection once, Glory: of the Samaritan Woman, Both now: Mid-Pentecost.
##### 4.2.15.2.4 Stichera of the Praises - 8: 5 Sunday and 3 of the Samaritan Woman with their two verses; Glory: of the Samaritan Woman, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou"
##### 4.2.15.2.5 After the Great Doxology - Sunday Troparion, one of the two
##### 4.2.15.2.6 Dismissal of Pascha, Glory, Both now: 7th Gospel Sticheron
#### 4.2.15.3 AT THE HOURS
At all: Sunday Troparion, Glory: Mid-Pentecost; Kontakia: at the 1st and 6th - of Mid-Pentecost, and at the 3rd and 9th - of the Samaritan Woman.
#### 4.2.15.4 AT THE LITURGY
##### 4.2.15.4.1 Antiphons, as every Sunday
##### 4.2.15.4.2 After the Entrance: Sunday Troparion and of Mid-Pentecost, Glory: Kontakion of the Samaritan Woman, Both now: of Mid-Pentecost. Everything else - only of the Sunday. Communion Hymn - "Receive the Body of Christ" and "Praise the Lord"
MONDAY AND TUESDAY IN THE MIDDLE OF
### 4.2.14 Mid-Pentecost
We sing the service of Mid-Pentecost and of the Menaion of the saint according to the general rubric of the weekdays of the Week of Thomas here, on page 424; specifically we take the troparia according to the general rubric in the middle of the feast just as we gave before, on page 434.
WEDNESDAY OF THE APODOSIS OF ### 4.2.14 Mid-Pentecost
We sing the whole service only of the feast, as on the day of the feast itself here, on page 434, except the Entrance and readings at Vespers and Epistle and Gospel at the Liturgy, which will be - of the day. The service of the saint of the Menaion we take at Compline.
THURSDAY, FRIDAY AND SATURDAY OF THE SAMARITAN WOMAN
We sing everything according to the rubric of the weekdays of the Paralytic here, on pp. 432-433 [→REF:p432-433].
### 4.2.16 Sunday of the Man Born Blind
Sixth from the Resurrection. Tone 5.
Everything - according to the rubric of the Sunday of the Paralytic here, on pp. 431-432 [→REF:p431-432]; only at Great Matins there will be the 8th Sunday Gospel and Katavasia - not of the Resurrection, but of the Ascension.
WEEKDAYS OF THE BLIND MAN
The service of the Blind Man continues only three weekdays: Monday, Tuesday and Wednesday, on which it is sung together with the Apodosis of the Resurrection and with the Forefeast of the Ascension.
MONDAY AND TUESDAY OF THE BLIND MAN
Everything - according to the rubric of the weekdays of the Paralytic here, on pp. 432-433 [→REF:p432-433].
WEDNESDAY OF THE APODOSIS OF THE RESURRECTION AT VESPERS
##### 4.2.16.1 On "Lord, I have cried" - stichera of the Blind Man on 6, Glory, Both now: His
##### 4.2.16.2 At the Aposticha: one Sunday sticheron, then Stichera of the Resurrection and "Christ is risen" once
##### 4.2.16.3 After "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart" - Troparion of the Sunday, Glory, Both now: Sunday Theotokion in His tone
##### 4.2.16.4 Dismissal of Pascha, intermediate, without the cross, with the commemoration of the saint of the day[^652]
AT COMPLINE: After "It is truly meet" - Kontakion of the Blind Man only.
AT THE Midnight Office
After the 1st Trisagion, instead of "Behold the Bridegroom", we read the Sunday Troparion "Let us the faithful praise", Glory, Both now: its Theotokion; after the 2nd Trisagion, instead of "Remember", we recite the Kontakion of the Resurrection; then "Lord, have mercy" (12) and dismissal, and the prayer "Remember, O Lord" we do not say.
AT MATINS
##### 4.2.16.5 On "God is the Lord": Sunday Troparion twice, Glory, Both now: its Theotokion
##### 4.2.16.6 After the 1st Kathisma - Sunday Sessional Hymn, after the 2nd - Sessional Hymn of the Blind Man and immediately "The Resurrection of Christ" once, Psalm 50 and Canon
##### 4.2.16.7 Canons 3 [making] 14: Resurrection with heirmos on 6, of the Blind Man on 4 and of the Forefeast of the Ascension on 4. Katavasia of the Ascension
##### 4.2.16.8 After the 3rd Ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Blind Man and his Sessional Hymn, Glory, Both now: of the Forefeast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Resurrection; at the 9th, instead of "More honorable", we sing "The angel cried" and "Shine, shine"; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the Resurrection twice, Glory, Both now: of the Blind Man
##### 4.2.16.9 Stichera of the Praises on 8: of the Blind Man on 4 and Stichera of the Resurrection with their verses; Glory, Both now: "The Day of Resurrection" and "Christ is risen" once
##### 4.2.16.10 After the Great Doxology - Troparion "Let us the faithful praise", Glory, Both now: its Theotokion; after the litanies - Great Dismissal, of Pascha, without the cross, with the commemoration of the saint of the day
AT THE HOURS: At all - Troparion "Let us the faithful praise" and Kontakion of the Blind Man[^653].
#### 4.2.16.1 AT THE LITURGY
After the Entrance: Troparion "Let us the faithful praise", Glory: Kontakion of the Blind Man, Both now: Kontakion of the Resurrection.
Apostle and Gospel of the day; everything else - just as on the day of the Resurrection itself. Dismissal - with the cross.
On Thursday of the sixth week from the Resurrection
### 4.2.17 Ascension of the Lord
This feast is celebrated for nine days, for it continues until the Friday after the Sunday of the Holy Fathers, on which it is leavetaken.
Feast, Afterfeast and Apodosis of the feast - according to the general rule of a feast of the Lord, only at the Liturgy our Sluzhebnyky, instead of "We have seen" and "Let our mouths be filled", give the verse "Be exalted, O God, above the heavens, and Thy glory over all the earth" and so we take until the Apodosis; on the following Sunday, of the Holy Fathers, the service of the Ascension has a specific rubric, for it is combined both with the Sunday service, and of the Holy Fathers, as is given below.
### 4.2.18 Sunday of the Holy Fathers of the First Nicaean Council
Seventh from the Resurrection. Tone 6.
#### 4.2.18.1 AT Great Vespers
##### 4.2.18.1.1 On "Lord, I have cried" - 10 stichera: 3 Sunday, 3 of the Ascension and 4 of the Fathers; Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: 1st Theotokion
##### 4.2.18.1.2 After "The Lord is King" - 3 readings of the Fathers
##### 4.2.18.1.3 Sunday Aposticha, Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: of the feast
##### 4.2.18.1.4 After "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart" - Sunday Troparion, Glory, Both now: of the feast
AT COMPLINE: After "It is truly meet" - Kontakion of the Fathers, Glory, Both now: Kontakion of the feast.
#### 4.2.18.2 AT Great Matins
##### 4.2.18.2.1 On "God is the Lord": Sunday Troparion twice, Glory, Both now: of the feast
##### 4.2.18.2.2 Usual Kathismata and everything else up to the canon - Sunday, as usual, only the Gospel will be the 10th Sunday
##### 4.2.18.2.3 Canons 3 [making] 14: Sunday with heirmos on 4, Ascension on 4 and of the Fathers on 6. Katavasia of the Descent
After the 3rd Ode - Kontakion-Ikos of the Ascension and Sessional Hymn of the Fathers, Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: of the Ascension; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the Fathers; at the 9th - "More honorable"; after "Holy is the Lord our God" - Sunday Exaposteilarion, Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: of the feast.
##### 4.2.18.2.4 Stichera of the Praises on 8: 4 Sunday and of the Fathers on 4; Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: "Most blessed Art Thou"
##### 4.2.18.2.5 After the Great Doxology - Sunday Troparion, one of the two usual, and after the dismissal - Glory, Both now: 6th Gospel Sticheron
#### 4.2.18.3 AT THE HOURS
At all: Sunday Troparion, Glory: at the 1st and 6th - also of the feast, and at the 3rd and 9th - to the Fathers.
Kontakia: at the 1st and 6th - of the feast, at the 3rd and 9th - to the Fathers.
#### 4.2.18.4 AT THE LITURGY
##### 4.2.18.4.1 After the Entrance: Sunday Troparion, of the feast and of the Fathers; Glory: Kontakion to the Fathers, Both now: of the feast
##### 4.2.18.4.2 Everything else - only to the Fathers, only Communion Hymn - "Praise the Lord" and to the Fathers ("Rejoice in the Lord, O ye righteous")
ON THE SUNDAY OF THE HOLY FATHERS IN THE EVENING
The service of the Holy Fathers is leavetaken according to the following rubric:
##### 4.2.18.4.3 On "Lord, I have cried" - 6 stichera: 3 of the Fathers and 3 of the saint of the Menaion, Glory: of the Fathers, Both now: of the feast
##### 4.2.18.4.4 At the Aposticha - Stichera of the Fathers, only Both now: of the feast
##### 4.2.18.4.5 After "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart" - Troparion of the Fathers, Glory: of the saint, Both now: of the feast. And thus the service of the Holy Fathers is leavetaken
On Friday after the Sunday of the Holy Fathers
APODOSIS OF THE ASCENSION
On Thursday evening and on Friday morning the service of the Ascension is sung according to the general rule of the Apodosis of the feast. The service of the saint is sung at Compline.
SOUL SATURDAY BEFORE THE DESCENT
Everything - according to the rubric of Meatfare Saturday here, on pp. 320-322 [→REF:p320-322].
### 4.2.19 Sunday of Pentecost
##### 4.2.19.1 The service of this feast is celebrated for seven days, for it is leavetaken on the following Saturday. And it has this peculiarity, that on Sunday evening at Vespers for Monday prayers with bending of knees are read, and in the morning, that is on Monday the whole service of the feast is sung, without the Menaion, and at the Liturgy - also the service of the Holy Trinity, if desired[^654], as we will note immediately. Everything else, from the feast to its Apodosis, is celebrated according to the general rule of the feast, in the middle of a feast of the Lord. At the Liturgy "All Who have been baptized into Christ" - only on the feast itself
##### 4.2.19.2 Here they have the custom on the day of Pentecost itself at the beginning of the Liturgy, after "Blessed is the Kingdom" (better before "Blessed is the Kingdom"), and afterwards, at the beginning of Vespers, after "Blessed is our God", to sing "O Heavenly King", bending the knees
##### 4.2.19.3 Vespers on the day of the feast itself we sing immediately after the Liturgy, in view of the prayers with bending of knees, so that all people may participate in them[^655]
##### 4.2.19.4 The service of a saint who falls on Sunday and on Monday, we sing on another day at Compline, by the decision of the Rector of the church
##### 4.2.19.5 Throughout this week at Compline - Canon of the Most Holy Theotokos, and also it is permitted to eat meat
### 4.2.20 Monday of the Holy Spirit
On Sunday evening
Note
The prayers with bending of knees are read in three places of Vespers: after the Great Prokimenon, after "Let us all say" and after "Vouchsafe, O Lord". At each of these three stations of prayers the Small Litany is sung, each time with a separate exclamation, however, thus: "Lord, have mercy" (3), then the Priest - one station of prayers with bending of knees; at the end of the station the Deacon or priest continues the Small Litany, that is: "Help us, save us, have mercy", "Commemorating our most holy, most pure", exclamation. So - at each station.
RUBRIC
AT VESPERS
##### 4.2.20.1 Immediately after the Liturgy the Priest and deacon, if there be one, dressed in all sacred vestments, standing at the altar, begin Vespers. Priest: "Blessed is our God" and, after "Amen", all, bending our knees, we sing "O Heavenly King", then we recite the Trisagion and the rest with "Our Father" and "O come, let us worship" (3), as usual
##### 4.2.20.2 Great Litany "In peace let us pray to the Lord" with the addition of specific petitions[^656]
##### 4.2.20.3 On "Lord, I have cried" - stichera of the feast on 6, Glory, Both now: "O Heavenly King"
##### 4.2.20.4 After the Entrance with the censer - Great Prokimenon of the feast
##### 4.2.20.5 After the singing of the Prokimenon the Deacon or priest, standing in the sanctuary, begins the Small Litany thus: "Again and again, bending our knees, let us pray to the Lord"; choir: "Lord, have mercy" (3) and, when all bend their knees, the Priest, kneeling outside the sanctuary, before the holy doors[^657], reads aloud, so that all may hear, two prayers with bending of knees; after the conclusion of them all rise, and the Deacon continues the Small Litany, saying: "Help us, save us"; choir: "Lord, have mercy" once. Deacon: "Commemorating our most holy, most pure"; priest - specific exclamation. We do exactly so after "Let us all say" and after "Vouchsafe"[^658]
##### 4.2.20.6 Aposticha of the feast; after "Now lettest Thou Thy servant depart" - Troparion of the feast and the Great Specific Dismissal of this Vespers
#### 4.2.20.1 AT COMPLINE
The Canon of the Theotokos is not taken, but the Canon of the Holy Spirit of the Triodion is sung with the refrain "Glory to Thee, our God, glory to Thee"; at the 9th Ode, instead of "It is truly meet", the heirmos of the same ode is taken, and after the Trisagion - Kontakion of the feast.
AT THE Midnight Office: After the 1st Trisagion - Troparion, and after the 2nd - Kontakion of the feast, without the prayer "Remember"[^659].
AT MATINS
##### 4.2.20.1.1 On "God is the Lord": Troparion of the feast three times
##### 4.2.20.1.2 After the two usual Kathismata - Sessional Hymns of the feast and immediately Psalm 50 and Canon
##### 4.2.20.1.3 Canons of the feast 2 [making] 14: first - with heirmos on 8, and second - with heirmos on 6. Katavasia of the feast
After the 3rd Ode - Sessional Hymn of the feast; after the 6th - Kontakion-Ikos of the feast; at the 9th "More honorable" we do not sing; after the 9th - Exaposteilarion of the feast, Glory, Both now: the second.
##### 4.2.20.1.4 Stichera of the Praises of the feast on 4; Glory, Both now: of the feast
##### 4.2.20.1.5 After the Great Doxology - Troparion of the feast; after the litanies - Great Dismissal of the feast "Who sent down from heaven"
AT THE HOURS: At all - Troparion and Kontakion of the feast.
#### 4.2.20.2 AT THE LITURGY
##### 4.2.20.2.1 Antiphons: two first - of the feast, third - daily with the refrain of the feast
##### 4.2.20.2.2 Entrance - specific; Troparion, Glory, Both now: Kontakion of the feast and Trisagion
##### 4.2.20.2.3 Prokimenon and Alleluia - specific; Apostle and Gospel - of the day; Communion Hymn - of the feast
Note
If the service of the Holy Trinity is also thought of, then after the Entrance, as we suppose (for there is nothing in the rubric), the whole service should be taken first of the Holy Trinity, then - of the Descent, Glory: Kontakion of the Holy Trinity, Both now: of the Descent; exactly so everything else: first - of the Holy Trinity, then - of Monday.
APODOSIS OF THE FEAST OF PENTECOST
Takes place on Saturday, according to the general rule of the Apodosis of a feast of the Lord.
